# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [Can SOC Operators Explain their Decisions while Triaging Alarms? A Real-World Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22001)
*Jessica Moosmann,Irdin Pekaric,Giovanni Apruzzese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述和实地研究发现，安全运营中心的分析师虽然能够正确区分真实告警与误报（83%的正确率），但仅有39%的决策能提供正确的理由说明，揭示出安全运营中决策可解释性的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心分析师在告警研判过程中需要基于知识和证据进行推理，但现有研究对其决策能否被合理证明（justify）关注不足。本文旨在探究：安全运营中心员工在研判告警时，能否为其决策提供合理的理由说明？

Method: 1) 对257篇研究文献进行系统性文献综述，验证该研究问题此前受关注程度；2) 与真实安全运营中心合作开展实地研究（n=12），向分析师展示真实告警，询问其是否属于真实安全问题并要求解释其决策。

Result: 分析师在83%的案例中能正确区分真实告警与误报，但仅有39%的解释能反映告警的真实根本原因。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了开发决策支持系统的必要性，这类系统不仅应帮助安全运营中心分析师做出正确判断，还应帮助他们理解和阐明判断正确的理由。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) are pivotal in modern enterprises. Tasked to monitor complex network environments constantly under attack, SOCs can be active 24/7 and can include hundreds of operators supported by state-of-the-art technologies. Abundant research has studied the internal processes of SOCs, highlighting their pros and cons, as well as the challenges faced by SOC analysts -- such as dealing with the overwhelming number of false alarms triggered by automated security mechanisms. In this context, we wonder: given that "someone" must triage the alarms, and that such triaging must be grounded on established knowledge or evidence-based reasoning, can SOC employees justify why a certain decision was taken while triaging alarms? Answering such a research question (RQ) can better guide future efforts.
  We hence tackle this RQs. First, via a systematic literature review across 257 research documents, we provide evidence that such RQ received limited attention so far. Then, we partner-up with a real-world SOC and carry out a field study (n=12) with SOC employees. We show them real alarms raised in their SOC, and inquire whether such alarms are indicative of true security problems or not. Then, we ask to explain their decision. We found that while most analysts were able to separate "true from false" alarms (the decision was correct in 83% of the cases), a correct justification was hardly provided (only 39% of the provided explanations reflected the actual root cause). Ultimately, our results highlight the need for decision-support systems that help SOC analysts not only make the right call -- but also understand and articulate why it is right.

</details>


### [70] [Sovereign Agentic Loops: Decoupling AI Reasoning from Execution in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22136)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Sovereign Agentic Loops (SAL)，一种控制平面架构，让LLM智能体发出结构化意图（附理由），控制平面在执行前验证意图与真实系统状态和策略是否一致。SAL通过混淆膜限制模型访问身份敏感状态，并利用密码学链接的证据链实现可审计和可重放。原型系统OpenKedge在云基础设施场景中，策略层阻断93%不安全意图，一致性检查拒绝剩余7%，基准测试中零不安全执行，中位延迟增加12.4ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体架构将随机模型输出直接传递给执行层，但模型的正确性、上下文感知和对齐在执行时不可靠，这种耦合造成了安全风险。

Method: SAL控制平面架构：模型输出结构化意图+理由；控制平面在执行前验证意图与真实系统状态和策略的一致性；混淆膜限制模型访问身份敏感状态；密码学链接的证据链实现审计和重放。

Result: OpenKedge原型：策略层阻断93%不安全意图，一致性检查拒绝剩余7%，基准测试零不安全执行，中位延迟仅增加12.4ms。

Conclusion: SAL在给定假设下提供了策略约束执行、身份隔离和确定性重放，有效缓解了LLM智能体的安全风险。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly issue API calls that mutate real systems, yet many current architectures pass stochastic model outputs directly to execution layers. We argue that this coupling creates a safety risk because model correctness, context awareness, and alignment cannot be assumed at execution time. We introduce Sovereign Agentic Loops (SAL), a control-plane architecture in which models emit structured intents with justifications, and the control plane validates those intents against true system state and policy before execution. SAL combines an obfuscation membrane, which limits model access to identity-sensitive state, with a cryptographically linked Evidence Chain for auditability and replay. We formalize SAL and show that, under the stated assumptions, it provides policy-bounded execution, identity isolation, and deterministic replay. In an OpenKedge prototype for cloud infrastructure, SAL blocks 93% of unsafe intents at the policy layer, rejects the remaining 7% via consistency checks, prevents unsafe executions in our benchmark, and adds 12.4 ms median latency.

</details>


### [71] [PrivSTRUCT: Untangling Data Purpose Compliance of Privacy Policies in Google Play Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22157)
*Bhanuka Silva,Anirban Mahanti,Aruna Seneviratne,Suranga Senevirante*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivSTRUCT框架，通过利用文档层级结构（章节标题）来解缠复杂隐私披露，提取的数据项和目的数量是现有方法的两倍以上。在3756款Android应用上的分析发现，开发者使用全局定义目的时，第一方收集和第三方共享的目的夸大概率分别高出20.4%和9.7%，且敏感第三方数据流常被稀释到通用或无关类别中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将隐私政策视为扁平化文本，忽略其逻辑层级结构（如章节标题的引导作用），导致自动化方法难以区分不同数据实践，尤其在关联敏感数据项与其特定目的时容易混淆。

Method: 提出PrivSTRUCT，一个结合编码器和解码器的系统化框架，利用开发者定义的文档结构线索（如章节标题）来解缠复杂隐私披露。

Result: 相比SOTA工具PoliGrapher，PrivSTRUCT提取的数据项和目的摘录数量翻倍以上（x2），且保留了结构线索。在3756款Android应用数据集中，发现全局定义目的比局部范围披露的目的夸大概率高（第一方收集高20.4%，第三方共享高9.7%），且敏感第三方数据流（如共享金融数据用于分析）常被稀释和纠缠到通用或无关类别中。

Conclusion: 当前隐私披露实践中存在严重的透明度差距，特别是第三方敏感数据流的目的披露存在系统性失败，PrivSTRUCT框架有效利用了文档结构信息来提升隐私披露分析的准确性和完整性。

Abstract: Existing research typically treats privacy policies as flat, uniform text, extracting information without regard for the document's logical hierarchy. Disregard for structural cues of section headings designed to guide the reader, often leads automated methods to entangle distinct data practices, particularly when linking sensitive data items to their specific purposes. To address this, we introduce PrivSTRUCT, a novel and systematic encoder and decoder combined framework that to untangle complex privacy disclosures. Benchmarking against the state-of-the-art tool PoliGrapher reveals that PrivSTRUCT robustly extracts more than x2 the number of data item and purpose excerpts while retaining developer-defined structural cues. By applying PrivSTRUCT to a large-scale dataset of 3,756 Android apps, we uncover a critical transparency gap: the probability of developers overstating a data purpose is 20.4% higher for first-party collection and 9.7% higher for third-party sharing when they rely on globally defined purposes rather than specific, locally scoped disclosures. Alarmingly, we find that sensitive third-party data flows such as sharing financial data for analytics are frequently diluted and entangled into generic or unrelated categories, highlighting a persistent failure in the current purpose disclosure landscape.

</details>


### [72] [FixV2W: Correcting Invalid CVE-CWE Mappings with Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22176)
*Sevval Simsek,Varsha Athreya,David Starobinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FixV2W是一种轻量级方法，利用知识图谱嵌入和纵向趋势分析，修正NVD中不准确或不完整的CVE到CWE映射。实验表明，该方法对已利用漏洞的正确映射预测率达69%，并将ML模型的MRR从0.174提升至0.608。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVD等公共数据库中的CVE到CWE映射存在不一致和不完整的问题，影响自动化漏洞分析和修复。

Method: 基于知识图谱嵌入和历史重映射模式分析，利用NVD与CWE之间的层次关系，为属于禁止或 discouraged 类别的漏洞预测更精确的CWE映射。

Result: 在Top-10预测中，对69%已利用漏洞正确预测了CWE映射；将ML模型（未知CVE-CWE映射发现任务）的MRR从0.174提升至0.608。

Conclusion: FixV2W是一种有前景的方法，可有效识别和阻止新兴威胁。

Abstract: Accurate mapping between Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) and Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) entries is critical for effective vulnerability management and risk assessment. However, public databases, such as the National Vulnerability Database (NVD), suffer from inconsistent and incomplete CVE to CWE mappings, complicating automated analysis and remediation. We introduce FixV2W, a lightweight approach that leverages knowledge graph embeddings and longitudinal trends to improve mapping accuracy of the NVD. FixV2W systematically analyzes historical remapping patterns and leverages hierarchical relationships within NVD and CWE data to predict more precise CWE mappings for vulnerabilities linked to Prohibited or Discouraged categories. We run extensive experimental evaluation of FixV2W, based on test data set collected between August 2021 and December 2024. Considering the Top 10 ranked predictions, the results show that FixV2W predicts the correct CWE mappings for 69% of exploited vulnerabilities that had invalid CWEs before they were exploited. We also show that FixV2W significantly improves the performance of ML models relying on NVD data. For instance, for a model geared at uncovering unknown CVE-CWE mappings, FixV2W improves the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) from 0.174 to 0.608. These results show that FixV2W is a promising approach to identify and thwart emerging threats.

</details>


### [73] [Train in Vain: Functionality-Preserving Poisoning to Prevent Unauthorized Use of Code Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22291)
*Yuan Xiao,Jiaming Wang,Yuchen Chen,Wei Song,Jun Sun,Shiqing Ma,Yanzhou Mu,Juan Zhai,Chunrong Fang,Jin Song Dong,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FunPoison是一种功能保持的代码数据集投毒方法，通过注入可编译的弱使用片段，仅污染10%的数据集即可有效防御未经授权的代码大模型训练，同时保持100%可编译性和功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码数据集投毒方法需要污染整个数据集，且引入的变换会破坏代码的可编译性，需要更高效且功能保持的投毒方法。

Method: FunPoison通过将可编译的弱使用片段注入到代码执行路径中，采用可复用的语句级模板配合自动修复和保守的安全性检查确保无副作用，同时使用类型感知合成模块抑制静态分析警告以增强隐蔽性。

Result: 仅污染10%的数据集即可实现有效投毒，保持100%可编译性和功能正确性，对多种先进的代码清洗技术具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FunPoison在低污染率下实现高效防御，且不影响代码质量和功能，为保护代码数据集版权提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The widespread availability of large-scale code datasets has accelerated the development of code large language models (CodeLLMs), raising concerns about unauthorized dataset usage. Dataset poisoning offers a proactive defense by reducing the utility of such unauthorized training. However, existing poisoning methods often require full dataset poisoning and introduce transformations that break code compilability. In this paper, we introduce FunPoison, a functionality-preserving poisoning approach that injects short, compilable weak-use fragments into executed code paths. FunPoison leverages reusable statement-level templates with automatic repair and conservative safety checking to ensure side-effect freedom, while a type-aware synthesis module suppresses static analysis warnings and enhances stealth. Extensive experiments show that FunPoison achieves effective poisoning by contaminating only 10% of the dataset, while maintaining 100% compilability and functional correctness, and remains robust against various advanced code sanitization techniques.

</details>


### [74] [Automation-Exploit: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Adaptive Offensive Security with Digital Twin-Based Risk-Mitigated Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22427)
*Biagio Andreucci,Arcangelo Castiglione*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Automation-Exploit，一个全自主的多智能体系统框架，用于黑盒场景下的自适应 offensive security。该框架通过自主渗出可执行文件和上下文信息，结合逻辑与二进制攻击链，并引入自适应安全架构缓解DoS风险。对高危内存破坏漏洞采用条件同构验证：若目标二进制成功渗出，则动态实例化跨平台数字孪生，通过强制状态同步（包括libc对齐和运行时文件描述符钩子）在隔离副本中迭代调试潜在破坏性负载，实现物理目标上的风险缓解型"一次性"执行。在八个场景（含未公开零日环境）中的实证评估验证了其架构韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 offensive security 领域高度碎片化：企业平台因DoS风险回避内存破坏漏洞，AEG系统存在语义盲区，LLM智能体面临安全对齐过滤和"实弹"执行风险。亟需一个能桥接侦察与利用之间的抽象鸿沟、同时有效缓解执行风险的全自主框架。

Method: 提出Automation-Exploit多智能体系统框架：(1) 自主跨协议渗出可执行文件与上下文情报；(2) 融合逻辑攻击链与二进制攻击链；(3) 自适应安全架构缓解DoS风险；(4) 对内存破坏漏洞采用条件同构验证——若目标二进制渗出成功，则动态创建跨平台数字孪生，通过libc对齐、文件描述符钩子等状态同步机制，在隔离副本中迭代调试payload，最终实现物理目标上的"一次性"低风险执行。

Result: 在八个场景中验证了框架有效性，包括未公开零日环境（排除LLM数据污染），证明了其能够防止"实弹"崩溃并在实际目标上执行风险缓解的入侵。

Conclusion: Automation-Exploit通过多智能体协作、数字孪生隔离调试和状态同步机制，有效解决了offensive security领域长期存在的碎片化问题，实现了高风险漏洞（如内存破坏）的风险可控利用，为自动化攻防提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: The offensive security landscape is highly fragmented: enterprise platforms avoid memory-corruption vulnerabilities due to Denial of Service (DoS) risks, Automatic Exploit Generation (AEG) systems suffer from semantic blindness, and Large Language Model (LLM) agents face safety alignment filters and "Live Fire" execution hazards. We introduce Automation-Exploit, a fully autonomous Multi-Agent System (MAS) framework designed for adaptive offensive security in complex black-box scenarios. It bridges the abstraction gap between reconnaissance and exploitation by autonomously exfiltrating executables and contextual intelligence across multiple protocols, using this data to fuel both logical and binary attack chains. The framework introduces an adaptive safety architecture to mitigate DoS risks. While it natively resolves logical and web-based vulnerabilities, it employs a conditional isomorphic validation for high-risk memory-corruption flaws: if the target binary is successfully exfiltrated, it dynamically instantiates a cross-platform digital twin. By enforcing strict state synchronization, including libc alignment and runtime file descriptor hooking, potentially destructive payloads are iteratively debugged in an isolated replica. This enables a highly risk-mitigated "one-shot" execution on the physical target. Empirical evaluations across eight scenarios, including undocumented zero-day environments to rule out LLM data contamination, validate the framework's architectural resilience, demonstrating its ability to prevent "live fire" crashes and execute risk-mitigated compromises on actual targets.

</details>


### [75] [Horizontal SCA Attacks on Binary kP Algorithms using Chevallier-Mames Atomic Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22429)
*Gerald Isheanesu Matungamire,Alkistis Aikaterini Sigourou,Gerrit Schrock,Zoya Dyka,Peter Langendoerfer,Ievgen Kabin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明使用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的二进制左右移kP算法，即使结合投影坐标随机化，仍易受单迹侧信道分析攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线密码系统中标量乘法kP是最频繁的操作，需要抵御单迹侧信道分析攻击，过去提出原子性原则和原子块模式来保护，但需要验证其有效性。

Method: 通过软件和硬件实现，对采用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的二进制右移和左移kP算法进行单迹SCA攻击测试，并检验左移算法结合投影坐标随机化的情况。

Result: 两种二进制kP算法（左右移）即使采用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式，仍然存在单迹SCA漏洞；左移算法在附加投影坐标随机化后仍未能抵御攻击。

Conclusion: Chevallier-Mames原子块模式不足以防护单迹SCA攻击，需要更安全的实现方案。

Abstract: Scalar multiplication kP is the operation most frequently targeted in Elliptic Curve (EC) cryptosystems. To protect against single-trace Side-Channel Analysis (SCA) attacks, the atomicity principle and various atomic block patterns have been proposed in the past. In this work we use our software and hardware implementations to demonstrate that binary right-to left and left-to-right kP algorithms, when implemented with Chevallier-Mames atomic block patterns, are still vulnerable to single-trace SCA attacks. The vulnerability remains true for the left-to-right kP algorithm with projective coordinate randomization.

</details>


### [76] [SSG: Logit-Balanced Vocabulary Partitioning for LLM Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22438)
*Chenxi Gu,Xiaoning Du,John Grundy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SSG方法，通过排序-分组-分割词汇表来提升KGW水印方案在低熵场景下的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGW水印方案在低熵场景（如代码生成、数学推理）下效果显著下降，原因在于随机词汇分割时水印强度受限于下一token概率分布的下界。

Method: 提出SSG（Sort-then-Split by Groups）方法，将词汇表划分为两个logit平衡的子集，从而提升每个token预测的水印强度下界。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理数据集上的实验证明了SSG的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计词汇分割算法可以提升水印强度的下界，SSG在低熵场景下显著改善了KGW水印的可检测性。

Abstract: Watermarking has emerged as a promising technique for tracing the authorship of content generated by large language models (LLMs). Among existing approaches, the KGW scheme is particularly attractive due to its versatility, efficiency, and effectiveness in natural language generation. However, KGW's effectiveness degrades significantly under low-entropy settings such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. A crucial step in the KGW method is random vocabulary partitioning, which enables adjustments to token selection based on specific preferences. Our study revealed that the next-token probability distribution plays an critical role in determining how much, or even whether, we can modify token selection and, consequently, the effectiveness of watermarking. We refer to this characteristic, associated with the probability distribution of each token prediction, as \emph{watermark strength.} In cases of random vocabulary partitioning, the lower bound of watermark strength is dictated by the next-token probability distribution. However, we found that, by redesigning the vocabulary partitioning algorithm, we can potentially raise this lower bound. In this paper, we propose SSG (\textbf{S}ort-then-\textbf{S}plit by \textbf{G}roups), a method that partitions the vocabulary into two logit-balanced subsets. This design lifts the lower bound of watermark strength for each token prediction, thereby improving watermark detectability. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SSG.

</details>


### [77] [Information-Theoretic Authenticated PIR: From PIR-RV To APIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22505)
*Pengzhen Ke,Yuxuan Qin,Liang Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息论安全认证隐私信息检索(itAPIR)的严格定义，证明了itPIR-RV方案可直接升级为itAPIR，无需额外开销，弥合了理论鸿沟并支持抗量子恶意服务器环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证PIR方案依赖计算困难假设，而信息论PIR结果验证方案虽无需计算假设，但仅提供松弛的查询隐私且无法防御选择性失败攻击。本文旨在构建无条件安全的itAPIR，填补理论空白。

Method: 提出itAPIR的严格信息论安全定义，包含统计隐私和完整性；形式化itAPIR与itPIR-RV的层次关系；证明转换定理：有效itPIR-RV方案可直接升级为安全itAPIR且无额外开销。

Result: 建立了itAPIR的严格安全定义；明确了itAPIR与itPIR-RV的包含关系；证明了itPIR-RV到itAPIR的零额外开销转换定理。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了理论鸿沟，简化了itAPIR设计，使得在恶意服务器环境下实现抗量子安全的PIR成为可能。

Abstract: Private Information Retrieval (PIR) allows clients to retrieve database entries without leaking retrieval indices, yet malicious servers seriously compromise retrieval correctness. Existing Authenticated PIR (APIR) schemes resist selective-failure attacks but rely on computational hardness assumptions. In contrast, information-theoretic PIR with Result Verification (itPIR-RV) achieves integrity without computational assumptions, yet only provides relaxed query privacy with no defense against selective-failure attacks. This paper focuses on unconditionally secure information-theoretic APIR (itAPIR) constructions. We propose the rigorous information-theoretic security definition for itAPIR with statistical privacy against selective-failure attacks and integrity as core properties, formalize the hierarchical relation between itAPIR and itPIR-RV as a relaxed variant with identical integrity but basic query privacy, and prove a conversion theorem that valid itPIR-RV schemes can be directly upgraded to secure itAPIR with no extra overhead. Our work bridges the theoretical gap, simplifies itAPIR design, and enables quantum-resistant PIR in malicious server environments.

</details>


### [78] [ArmSSL: Adversarial Robust Black-Box Watermarking for Self-Supervised Learning Pre-trained Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22550)
*Yongqi Jiang,Yansong Gao,Boyu Kuang,Chunyi Zhou,Anmin Fu,Liquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ArmSSL框架，通过配对差异扩大、潜在表示纠缠和分布对齐等技术，在保持可用性的同时，实现对自监督学习编码器的黑盒可验证水印和对抗鲁棒性保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习编码器作为重要知识产权，现有水印方案无法同时满足两个实际需求：（1）窃取编码器用于下游任务时，能在黑盒访问下提供所有权验证；（2）水印样本易形成可区分的分布外簇，对对抗性水印检测或去除缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: （1）配对差异扩大：强制干净样本与水印样本在特征空间中正交，产生可靠的黑盒验证信号；（2）潜在表示纠缠：将水印表示与非源类别的干净表示纠缠，避免形成密集水印簇；（3）分布对齐：最小化水印与干净表示之间的分布差异，伪装水印为自然分布内数据；（4）参考引导水印微调：通过将水印编码器输出与原始干净编码器在正常数据上对齐，使水印作为小侧任务学习。

Result: 在5种主流SSL框架和9个基准数据集上的大量实验，以及与SOTA的端到端比较表明，ArmSSL实现了优越的所有权验证、可忽略的可用性损失，以及对各种对抗性检测和去除的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ArmSSL有效解决了SSL编码器IP保护中黑盒可验证性与对抗鲁棒性的权衡问题，通过创新的特征空间操作和分布伪装策略，为自监督模型水印提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) encoders are invaluable intellectual property (IP). However, no existing SSL watermarking for IP protection can concurrently satisfy the following two practical requirements: (1) provide ownership verification capability under black-box suspect model access once the stolen encoders are used in downstream tasks; (2) be robust under adversarial watermark detection or removal, because the watermark samples form a distinguishable out-of-distribution (OOD) cluster. We propose ArmSSL, an SSL watermarking framework that assures black-box verifiability and adversarial robustness while preserving utility. For verification, we introduce paired discrepancy enlargement, enforcing feature-space orthogonality between the clean and its watermark counterpart to produce a reliable verification signal in black-box against the suspect model. For adversarial robustness, ArmSSL integrates latent representation entanglement and distribution alignment to suppress the OOD clustering. The former entangles watermark representations with clean representations (i.e., from non-source-class) to avoid forming a dense cluster of watermark samples, while the latter minimizes the distributional discrepancy between watermark and clean representations, thereby disguising watermark samples as natural in-distribution data. For utility, a reference-guided watermark tuning strategy is designed to allow the watermark to be learned as a small side task without affecting the main task by aligning the watermarked encoder's outputs with those of the original clean encoder on normal data. Extensive experiments across five mainstream SSL frameworks and nine benchmark datasets, along with end-to-end comparisons with SOTAs, demonstrate that ArmSSL achieves superior ownership verification, negligible utility degradation, and strong robustness against various adversarial detection and removal.

</details>


### [79] [Adversarial Co-Evolution of Malware and Detection Models: A Bilevel Optimization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22569)
*Olha Jurečková,Martin Jureček,Matouš Kozák,Róbert Lórencz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于双层优化的鲁棒防御框架，通过对抗性共同进化过程建模攻防交互，在MAB-malware框架下对三种恶意软件家族的测试中，将逃逸率从90%降至0-1.89%，并将攻击者查询成本提升两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的恶意软件检测器日益受到对抗性样本的威胁，而传统防御方法（如一次性对抗训练）在面对使用强化学习的自适应攻击者时往往失效。

Method: 提出基于双层优化的鲁棒防御框架，显式地将防御者与攻击者之间的战略交互建模为一个对抗性共同进化过程。

Result: 标准分类器和基础对抗重训练的逃逸率高达90%，而双层优化方法将逃逸率降至0-1.89%，并将攻击者成功逃逸的平均成本提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过双层优化建模攻防迭代循环，对于开发能够抵御不断演化的对抗性威胁的弹性恶意软件检测系统至关重要。

Abstract: Machine learning-based malware detectors are increasingly vulnerable to adversarial examples. Traditional defenses, such as one-shot adversarial training, often fail against adaptive attackers who use reinforcement learning to bypass detection. This paper proposes a robust defense framework based on bilevel optimization, explicitly modeling the strategic interaction between a defender and an attacker as an adversarial co-evolutionary process. We evaluate our approach using the MAB-malware framework against three distinct malware families: Mokes, Strab, and DCRat. Our experimental results demonstrate that while standard classifiers and basic adversarial retraining often remain vulnerable, showing evasion rates as high as 90 %, the proposed bilevel optimization approach consistently achieves near-total immunity, reducing evasion rates to 0 - 1.89 %. Furthermore, the iterative framework significantly increases the attacker's query complexity, raising the average cost of successful evasion by up to two orders of magnitude. These findings suggest that modeling the iterative cycle of attack and defense through bilevel optimization is essential for developing resilient malware detection systems capable of withstanding evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [80] [Detecting Concept Drift in Evolving Malware Families Using Rule-Based Classifier Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22629)
*Tomáš Kalný,Martin Jureček,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策树规则集的结构化概念漂移检测方法，在EMBER2024数据集上通过特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性和覆盖度等指标量化漂移，并与准确率下降和数据分布变化相关联。实验表明固定两个月窗口结合特征级皮尔逊相关是最可靠配置，所有家族对的漂移-准确率相关性均为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件分类中的概念漂移检测方法多依赖黑盒性能指标或数据分布统计，缺乏对分类器内部决策逻辑结构变化的可解释性分析，且未充分探索规则级表征在漂移检测中的价值。

Method: 基于决策树规则集提取漂移指标（特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性、覆盖度），采用固定间隔和基于聚类的滑动窗口策略，在六种恶意软件家族上比较家族vs良性及家族vs家族设置，并对比RIPPER和Transcendent基线。

Result: 固定两个月窗口结合特征级皮尔逊相关在所有家族对上均产生正向漂移-准确率相关性；不同方法在不同场景下表现互补，无单一方法在所有场景中占优。

Conclusion: 基于规则结构的概念漂移检测是有效且可解释的方法，固定滑动窗口与特征级相关性分析在实际部署中具有最佳可靠性，多种检测方法联合使用可互补提升漂移监测效果。

Abstract: This work proposes a structural approach to concept drift detection in malware classification using decision tree rulesets. Classifiers are trained across temporal windows on the EMBER2024 dataset, and drift is quantified by comparing extracted rule representations using feature importance, prediction agreement, activation stability, and coverage metrics. These metrics are correlated with both accuracy degradation and data distribution shift as complementary drift indicators. The approach is evaluated across six malware families using fixed-interval and clustering-based windowing in family-vs-benign and family-vs-family settings, and compared against RIPPER and Transcendent baselines. Results show that fixed two-month windowing with feature-level Pearson correlation is the most reliable configuration, being the only one where all family pairs produce positive drift-accuracy correlations. The methods are complementary - no single approach dominates across all pairs.

</details>


### [81] [Adversarial Malware Generation in Linux ELF Binaries via Semantic-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22639)
*Lukáš Hrdonka,Martin Jureček*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对Linux ELF格式的恶意软件，开发了一种新的对抗性生成器，通过注入良性文件特征字符串，成功使MalConv检测器的逃逸率达到67.74%，并发现该分类器对可执行文件中任意位置的字符串均敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于Windows PE文件的对抗性恶意软件生成与检测，而对Linux ELF格式的研究相对匮乏。本文旨在填补这一空白，针对ELF文件开发对抗性生成器并评估其有效性。

Method: 总结已有学术论文，开发新型ELF格式对抗性恶意软件生成器；以MalConv为目标分类器，采用良性文件典型字符串作为数据源进行修改；使用多种指标（逃逸率、置信度变化等）评估生成器性能。

Result: 生成器实现67.74%的逃逸率，平均将检测器置信度降低-0.50；最成功的修改方式为使用良性文件中的字符串；实验表明目标分类器对文件中任意位置的字符串都敏感。

Conclusion: 基于字符串注入的对抗性方法在ELF格式上有效，且MalConv对字符串位置不敏感，为后续ELF对抗样本研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Malware development and detection have undergone significant changes in recent years as modern concepts, such as machine learning, have been used for both adversarial attacks and defense. Despite intensive research on Windows Portable Executable (PE) files, there is minimal work on Linux Executable and Linkable Format (ELF). In this work, we summarize the academic papers submitted in this field and develop a new adversarial malware generator for the ELF format. Using a variety of metrics, we thoroughly evaluated our generator and achieved an Evasion Rate of 67.74 % while changing the confidence of the malware detector by -0.50 in the mean case for the dataset used. In our approach, we chose MalConv as the target classifier. Using this classifier, we found that the most successful modifications used strings typical of benign files as a data source. We conducted a variety of experiments and concluded that the target classifier appears sensitive to strings at any location within the executable file.

</details>

### [82] [Concave Statistical Utility Maximization Bandits via Influence-Function Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22140)
*Matías Carrasco,Alejandro Cholaquidis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究以长期回报分布的统计泛函（而非仅期望回报）为目标的多臂赌博机问题。在温和连续性假设下，证明无限时域问题可简化为对平稳混合策略的优化，并提出基于影响函数微分的熵镜像上升算法，通过乘性权重更新和影响函数插件估计实现，给出了分离优化误差与估计误差的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多臂赌博机研究仅关注期望回报，无法处理方差、分位数等更丰富的分布统计泛函目标，因此需要发展面向一般分布性统计效用的框架。

Method: 利用影响函数微分为可微统计效用推导随机梯度估计器；在截断单纯形上执行熵镜像上升算法，通过乘性权重更新和影响函数的插件估计实现；理论分析将镜像上升的优化误差与影响函数估计的偏差分离。

Result: 建立了可分离优化误差与估计误差的遗憾界；通过方差和Wasserstein目标函数进行了数值实验，对比了精确影响函数与插件影响函数实现的效果。

Conclusion: 提出的框架可推广至一般凹分布性效用，为超越期望回报的分布目标多臂赌博机问题提供了理论保证与实用算法。

Abstract: We study stochastic multi-armed bandits in which the objective is a statistical functional of the long-run reward distribution, rather than expected reward alone. Under mild continuity assumptions, we show that the infinite-horizon problem reduces to optimizing over stationary mixed policies: each weight vector \(w\) on the simplex induces a mixture law \(P^w\), and performance is measured by the concave utility \(U(w)=\mathfrak U(P^w)\).
  For differentiable statistical utilities, we use influence-function calculus to derive stochastic gradient estimators from bandit feedback. This leads to an entropic mirror-ascent algorithm on a truncated simplex, implemented through multiplicative-weights updates and plug-in estimates of the influence function. We establish regret bounds that separate the mirror-ascent optimization error from the bias caused by estimating the influence function. The framework is developed for general concave distributional utilities and illustrated through variance and Wasserstein objectives, with numerical experiments comparing exact and plug-in influence-function implementations.

</details>


### [83] [Pliable rejection sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22385)
*Akram Erraqabi,Michal Valko,Alexandra Carpentier,Odalric-Ambrym Maillard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为可塑拒绝采样（PRS）的新方法，通过核估计器学习采样提议分布，在保持拒绝采样理论保证的同时，提高了采样效率并提供了接受样本数量的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拒绝采样方法因高拒绝率而应用受限，现有的自适应拒绝采样方法要么仅适用于特定分布，要么缺乏性能保证。因此，需要一种既能提高采样效率又具有理论保证的通用方法。

Method: PRS方法使用核估计器（kernel estimator）学习采样提议分布，并基于拒绝采样框架生成样本。通过学习目标分布的近似，构建更高效的提议分布以减少拒绝率。

Result: 生成的样本以高概率独立同分布且服从目标分布f；PRS方法提供关于接受样本数量的理论保证。

Conclusion: PRS在保持拒绝采样原始优势（生成i.i.d.样本）的同时，通过数据驱动的方式学习提议分布，显著扩展了拒绝采样的适用性，并提供了可量化的性能保障。

Abstract: Rejection sampling is a technique for sampling from difficult distributions. However, its use is limited due to a high rejection rate. Common adaptive rejection sampling methods either work only for very specific distributions or without performance guarantees. In this paper, we present pliable rejection sampling (PRS), a new approach to rejection sampling, where we learn the sampling proposal using a kernel estimator. Since our method builds on rejection sampling, the samples obtained are with high probability i.i.d. and distributed according to f. Moreover, PRS comes with a guarantee on the number of accepted samples.

</details>


### [84] [Pack only the essentials: Adaptive dictionary learning for kernel ridge regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22386)
*Daniele Calandriello,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SQUEAK算法，通过使用非标准化RLS解决了KRR中Nyström近似的高空间复杂度问题，相比INK-Estimate算法更简单且空间复杂度仅比精确RLS采样差常数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核岭回归（KRR）中存储核矩阵需要O(n²)空间，Nyström近似虽可降低空间复杂度，但均匀采样在数据高相干性时需要O(n)列；基于岭杠杆分数（RLS）的采样虽更优，但计算精确RLS仍需O(n²)空间；现有增量算法INK-Estimate引入了对最大特征值的依赖，最坏情况下仍为O(n)。因此需要更高效的算法。

Method: 提出SQUEAK算法，在INK-Estimate基础上使用非标准化（unnormalized）RLS，避免了有效维度的估计和归一化步骤，简化了算法流程。

Result: SQUEAK的空间复杂度仅比精确RLS采样差一个常数因子，且消除了INK-Estimate中对最大特征值的O(n)依赖。

Conclusion: SQUEAK是一种更简单、空间效率更高的Nyström近似采样算法，适用于大规模KRR问题。

Abstract: One of the major limits of kernel ridge regression (KRR) is that storing and manipulating the kernel matrix K_n for n samples requires O(n^2) space, which rapidly becomes unfeasible for large n. Nystrom approximations reduce the space complexity to O(nm) by sampling m columns from K_n. Uniform sampling preserves KRR accuracy (up to epsilon) only when m is proportional to the maximum degree of freedom of K_n, which may require O(n) columns for datasets with high coherence. Sampling columns according to their ridge leverage scores (RLS) gives accurate Nystrom approximations with m proportional to the effective dimension, but computing exact RLS also requires O(n^2) space.
  (Calandriello et al. 2016) propose INK-Estimate, an algorithm that processes the dataset incrementally and updates RLS, effective dimension, and Nystrom approximations on-the-fly. Its space complexity scales with the effective dimension but introduces a dependency on the largest eigenvalue of K_n, which in the worst case is O(n).
  In this paper we introduce SQUEAK, a new algorithm that builds on INK-Estimate but uses unnormalized RLS. As a consequence, the algorithm is simpler, does not need to estimate the effective dimension for normalization, and achieves a space complexity that is only a constant factor worse than exact RLS sampling.

</details>


### [85] [Conformalized Super Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22391)
*Zhanli Wu,Fabrizio Leisen,Miguel-Angel Luque-Fernandez,F. Javier Rubio*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将共形预测（CP）与超级学习器（SL）结合，通过加权多数投票整合各学习器的共形分数，构建具有有限样本覆盖保证的预测区间，并在模拟和实际数据（肌酐水平预测）中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级学习器（SL）虽广泛用于点预测集成，但其区间预测方法或依赖渐近论证，或需计算密集的bootstrap。本文旨在将共形预测（CP）这一具有有限样本覆盖保证的框架与SL结合，以构建更可靠的预测区间。

Method: 采用与原始SL框架自然对应的构造方式，利用个体学习器权重，通过加权多数投票整合各学习器特定的共形分数（conformity scores），从而生成预测区间。方法覆盖可交换性、可交换性违反、异方差、稀疏性等复杂数据生成机制。

Result: 模拟研究表明，共形化超级学习器在有限样本下实现了有效的覆盖，性能与真实数据生成机制相当。实际应用（预测肌酐水平）展示了该方法在处理非线性、交互效应、稀疏性、异方差和异常值鲁棒性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 共形预测与超级学习器的结合提供了一种计算高效且具有理论保证的区间预测方法，适用于复杂回归场景，在生物医学等领域的预测不确定性量化中具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: The Super Learner (SL) is a widely used ensemble method that combines predictions from a library of learners based on their predictive performance. Interval predictions are of considerable practical interest because they allow uncertainty in predictions produced by an individual learner or an ensemble to be quantified. Several methods have been proposed for constructing interval predictions based on the SL, however, these approaches are typically justified using asymptotic arguments or rely on computationally intensive procedures such as the bootstrap. Conformal prediction (CP) is a machine learning framework for constructing prediction intervals with finite-sample and asymptotic coverage guarantees under mild conditions. We propose coupling CP with the SL through a natural construction that mirrors the original SL framework, using individual learner weights and combining learner-specific conformity scores via a weighted majority vote. We characterize the properties of the resulting SL-based prediction intervals for continuous outcomes. We cover settings under exchangeability, potential violations of exchangeability, and data-generating mechanisms exhibiting heteroscedasticity, sparsity, and other forms of distributional heterogeneity. A comprehensive simulation study shows that the conformalized SL achieves valid finite-sample coverage with competitive performance relative to the true data-generating mechanism. A central contribution of this work is an application to predicting creatinine levels using socio-demographic, biometric, and laboratory measurements. This example demonstrates the benefits of an ensemble with carefully selected learners designed to capture key aspects of complex regression functions, including non-linear effects, interactions, sparsity, heteroscedasticity, and robustness to outliers.R

</details>


### [86] [FedSPDnet: Geometry-Aware Federated Deep Learning with SPDnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22494)
*Thibault Pautrel,Florent Bouchard,Ammar Mian,Guillaume Ginolhac*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种联邦学习框架（ProjAvg和RLAvg），用于处理对称正定矩阵上的SPDnet模型，通过保持Stiefel流形上的几何结构实现高效聚合，在EEG运动想象基准上优于联邦EEGnet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准欧氏平均会破坏Stiefel约束的正交性，现有方法缺乏几何感知的联邦聚合策略。

Method: 提出ProjAvg（将算术平均投影到Stiefel流形）和RLAvg（通过收缩和提升近似切空间平均）两种聚合策略，独立于优化器，计算高效。

Result: 在EEG运动想象基准上，FedSPDnet在F1分数和鲁棒性方面优于联邦EEGnet，且每轮通信参数更少。

Conclusion: 几何感知的聚合策略能有效保护流形结构，为SPD矩阵特征的信号处理应用提供可扩展的联邦学习方案。

Abstract: We introduce two federated learning frameworks for the classical SPDnet model operating on symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices with Stiefel-constrained parameters. Unlike standard Euclidean averaging, which violates orthogonality, our approach preserves geometric structure through two efficient aggregation strategies: ProjAvg, projecting arithmetic means onto the Stiefel manifold, and RLAvg, approximating tangent-space averaging via retractions and liftings. Both methods are computationally efficient, independent of the optimizer, and enable scalable federated learning for signal processing applications whose features are SPD matrices. Simulations on EEG motor imagery benchmarks show that FedSPDnet outperforms federated EEGnet in F1 score and robustness to federation and partial participation, while using fewer parameters per communication round.

</details>

### [87] [Minimax Robust Designs for M-Estimated Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21998)
*Rui Hu,Douglas P. Wiens*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文扩展了存在模型误设定时的极小极大实验设计理论，从最小二乘估计推广至M估计，证明最小二乘最优设计在M估计下渐近保持最优性（仅需微调一个常数），并建议忽略该调整而采用与具体M估计无关的启发式常数。所提设计在独立同分布误差假设下推导，对广泛的误差相关结构也具有极小极大鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极小极大设计理论仅针对最小二乘估计，缺乏对异常响应具有鲁棒性的M估计的设计方法。

Method: 将极小极大设计理论从最小二乘推广至M估计，分析调优常数的影响，并检验设计对误差相关结构的鲁棒性。

Result: 最小二乘最优设计在M估计下渐近最优，仅需微小调优常数调整；该调整可忽略，采用与M估计无关的启发式常数即可；设计对广泛相关结构具有极小极大鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 面向最小二乘的极小极大设计可直接用于M估计，且对误差相关性具有鲁棒性，实现了对模型误设定和异常响应的双重稳健性。

Abstract: Experimental designs that are minimax in the presence of model misspecifications have been constructed so as to minimize the maximum, over classes of alternate response models, of the integrated mean squared error of the predicted values. The theory to date has focussed almost exclusively on Least Squares estimates. Here we extend this theory to designs tailored for M-estimation of parameters, thus obtaining additional robustness against outlying responses. We show that, subject to a minor change in a tuning constant, designs optimal for Least Squares remain so asymptotically for M-estimation. We argue that even this minor change should be ignored, and the tuning constant chosen in an ad hoc but sensible manner which does not depend on which M-estimate is being employed. Our designs and estimates, derived under an assumption of i.i.d. errors, are also shown to be robust, in a minimax sense, against broad classes of correlation structures.

</details>


### [88] [Laplace Transform driven Stein-type Goodness-of-fit Tests for Pareto Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22486)
*Deepesh Bhati,Sakshi Khandelwal*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文基于Stein特征和拉普拉斯变换提出了一种新的Pareto分布拟合优度检验方法，理论证明了渐近性质，实证表明该方法在检验功效上优于或等同于现有方法，并通过实际数据验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pareto分布在多个学科中具有重要作用，需要稳健的拟合优度检验方法来验证模型的有效性，现有方法存在改进空间。

Method: 基于Stein特征和拉普拉斯变换构造新的检验统计量，并建立其渐近性质。

Result: 新方法在检验功效上通常优于或与已有方法相当；通过实际数据应用展示了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新颖且有效的Pareto分布拟合优度检验方法，具有良好的渐近性质和实证表现。

Abstract: The Pareto distribution plays a crucial role in various disciplines, necessitating robust goodness-of-fit tests for its validation. This article introduces a novel tests based on Stein's characterization and the Laplace transform, offering a fresh perspective on model assessment. We establish the asymptotic properties of the proposed test and evaluate its empirical performance against existing methods in terms of size and power. Our findings demonstrate that the new test often outperforms or performs comparably to established tests. In addition, real data applications illustrate its practical utility.

</details>


### [89] [Sharp bounds for products of dependent random variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22667)
*Christopher Blier-Wong,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了当单变量边际分布已知但依赖结构未指定时，乘积期望E[X₁···X_d]的尖锐上下界。针对变量可取正负值的情况，将问题分解为幅度部分和符号部分，提出通过绝对值共单调耦合与适当符号向量得到通用界，并给出界可达的充要条件（上界对应偶校验多面体，下界对应奇校验多面体），构造了极值耦合，并在三维及更高维中显式构造了极值copula。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在非负变量情形下通过共单调耦合可直接最大化乘积期望，但当变量可取正负值时问题更为复杂（两个负值相乘得正，单个负值得负），需要新的分解方法给出乘积期望的通用上下界并确定可达性条件。

Method: 将问题分解为幅度部分（绝对值）和符号部分；使用绝对值共单调耦合处理幅度，通过奇/偶校验多面体刻画符号向量条件；利用可测选择构造极值耦合；采用递归奇偶分解从三维copula构造高维极值copula。

Result: 给出了乘积期望通用上下界可达的充要条件：上界对应偶校验多面体，下界对应奇校验多面体；构造了满足条件的极值耦合；在三维中显式构造了极值copula，并通过递归奇偶分解扩展到高维。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了已知边际分布下乘积期望的极值边界问题，将符号依赖结构归结为奇偶校验多面体条件，并提供了显式的极值copula构造方法，为处理含负值变量的依赖极值问题提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study the sharp bounds of $\mathbb{E}[X_1\cdots X_d]$ when the univariate marginal distributions are known, but the dependence structure between them is unspecified. Maximizing products over non-negative variables is straightforward via the comonotonic coupling, but the problem is more subtle when the marginals can take both positive and negative values. Specifically, two negative realizations can be matched to yield a positive product, whereas a single negative realization necessarily yields a negative product. We propose a decomposition of the problem into a magnitude part and a sign part, and show that universal upper and lower bounds for the product expectation follow from the comonotonic coupling of the absolute values and properly chosen sign vectors. Under a mild regularity assumption, we give necessary and sufficient conditions for these universal bounds to be attainable. For the upper bound, the marginal sign-bias vector must belong to the even-parity polytope, while for the lower, the corresponding condition involves the odd-parity polytope. We construct the extremal couplings via measurable selections on the parity polytope whenever these conditions hold. We study the case of identical marginals in more detail and provide examples of non-symmetric extremal coupling that achieve the universal bounds. We explicitly construct the extremal copulas in three dimensions, and use a recursive parity decomposition to obtain higher-dimensional extremal copulas from the trivariate ones.

</details>

### [90] [Analyzing Shapley Additive Explanations to Understand Anomaly Detection Algorithm Behaviors and Their Complementarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00208)
*Jordan Levy,Paul Saves,Moncef Garouani,Nicolas Verstaevel,Benoit Gaudou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SHAP解释来量化异常检测器的决策机制相似性，基于解释多样性构建互补性更强的集成模型，从而提升无监督异常检测的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测中，集成方法常因检测器依赖相似决策线索而产生冗余分数，难以实现真正的互补，限制了集成学习的潜力。

Method: 使用SHAP值量化每个检测器对输入特征的归因重要性，通过解释画像衡量检测器间的相似性，并基于解释多样性选择互补的检测器构建集成。

Result: 解释相似的检测器产生相关异常分数并识别重叠异常；解释分歧可靠地指示互补检测行为。同时证明仅多样性不够，高个体性能是有效集成的先决条件。

Conclusion: 通过显式追求解释多样性并保持模型质量，可以构建更多样、更互补且更有效的无监督异常检测集成模型。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is a challenging problem due to the diversity of data distributions and the lack of labels. Ensemble methods are often adopted to mitigate these challenges by combining multiple detectors, which can reduce individual biases and increase robustness. Yet building an ensemble that is genuinely complementary remains challenging, since many detectors rely on similar decision cues and end up producing redundant anomaly scores. As a result, the potential of ensemble learning is often limited by the difficulty of identifying models that truly capture different types of irregularities. To address this, we propose a methodology for characterizing anomaly detectors through their decision mechanisms. Using SHapley Additive exPlanations, we quantify how each model attributes importance to input features, and we use these attribution profiles to measure similarity between detectors. We show that detectors with similar explanations tend to produce correlated anomaly scores and identify largely overlapping anomalies. Conversely, explanation divergence reliably indicates complementary detection behavior. Our results demonstrate that explanation-driven metrics offer a different criterion than raw outputs for selecting models in an ensemble. However, we also demonstrate that diversity alone is insufficient; high individual model performance remains a prerequisite for effective ensembles. By explicitly targeting explanation diversity while maintaining model quality, we are able to construct ensembles that are more diverse, more complementary, and ultimately more effective for unsupervised anomaly detection.

</details>


### [91] [Focus Session: Hardware and Software Techniques for Accelerating Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21952)
*Muhammad Shafique,Abdul Basit,Muhammad Abdullah Hanif,Alberto Marchisio,Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Minghao Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多层方法学，通过硬件软件协同设计、混合精度量化、结构剪枝、推测解码、模型级联、序列长度/分辨率/步长协同优化、算子融合、数据流优化及专用硬件加速器，高效加速多模态基础模型，并在医疗和代码生成任务上验证有效性，拓展至节能脉冲多模态基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型（MFMs）在计算和内存上开销巨大，亟需系统化的加速方法以提高效率。

Method: 1) 硬件-软件协同设计的Transformer模块与优化流水线；2) 领域自适应微调；3) 层级感知混合精度量化与结构剪枝压缩；4) 推测解码、小到大模型级联路由与轻量级自测试；5) 序列长度、视觉分辨率与步长、图级算子融合的协同优化；6) 基于硬件架构的数据流优化与内存高效注意力；7) 专用Transformer硬件加速器（专家设计或LLM辅助设计）。

Result: 在医疗多模态基础模型和代码生成任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法论可显著加速多模态基础模型，并拓展至节能脉冲多模态基础模型方向。

Abstract: This work presents a multi-layered methodology for efficiently accelerating multimodal foundation models (MFMs). It combines hardware and software co-design of transformer blocks with an optimization pipeline that reduces computational and memory requirements. During model development, it employs performance enhancements through fine-tuning for domain-specific adaptation. Our methodology further incorporates hardware and software techniques for optimizing MFMs. Specifically, it employs MFM compression using hierarchy-aware mixed-precision quantization and structural pruning for transformer blocks and MLP channels. It also optimizes operations through speculative decoding, model cascading that routes queries through a small-to-large cascade and uses lightweight self-tests to determine when to escalate to larger models, as well as co-optimization of sequence length, visual resolution & stride, and graph-level operator fusion. To efficiently execute the model, the processing dataflow is optimized based on the underlying hardware architecture together with memory-efficient attention to meet on-chip bandwidth and latency budgets. To support this, a specialized hardware accelerator for the transformer workloads is employed, which can be developed through expert design or an LLM-aided design approach. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methodology on medical-MFMs and on code generation tasks, and conclude with extensions toward energy-efficient spiking-MFMs.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-Task Optimization over Networks of Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21991)
*Julian Hatzky,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein,A. E. Eiben,Anil Yaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MONET是一种将任务空间建模为图结构的多任务优化算法，通过社会学习（邻居交叉）和个体学习（自身突变）实现知识迁移，在四个领域（射箭、机械臂、cartpole各5000任务，六足机器人2000任务）上匹配或超越现有MAP-Elites基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于种群的多任务优化方法在任务数量大时扩展性差，而能扩展到上千任务的MAP-Elites变体依赖固定的离散化存档，忽略了任务空间的拓扑结构，导致知识迁移效率受限。

Method: 将任务空间建模为图：任务作为节点，任务参数空间中的连接作为边。采用社会学习（从邻居节点通过交叉生成候选解）和个体学习（通过突变独立优化自身解）相结合的机制。

Result: 在射箭、机械臂、cartpole（各5000任务）和六足机器人（2000任务）四个领域的实验中，MONET在所有域上匹配或超越了现有MAP-Elites基线的性能。

Conclusion: 图结构表示使得任务拓扑得以利用，在保持高维问题可解性的同时实现高效知识迁移，为大规模多任务优化提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Multi-task optimization is a powerful approach for solving a large number of tasks in parallel. However, existing algorithms face distinct limitations: Population-based methods scale poorly and remain underexplored for large task sets. Approaches that do scale beyond a thousand tasks are mostly MAP-Elites variants and rely on a fixed, discretized archive that disregards the topology of the task space. We introduce MONET (Multi-Task Optimization over Networks of Tasks), a multi-task optimization algorithm that models the task space as a graph: tasks are nodes, and edges connect tasks in the task parameter space. This representation enables knowledge transfer between tasks and remains tractable for high-dimensional problems while exploiting the topology of the task space. MONET combines social learning, which generates candidates from neighboring nodes via crossover, with individual learning, which refines a node's own solution independently via mutation. We evaluate MONET on four domains (archery, arm, and cartpole with 5,000 tasks each; hexapod with 2,000 tasks) and show that it matches or exceeds the performance of existing MAP-Elites-based baselines across all four domains.

</details>


### [93] [Universal Transformers Need Memory: Depth-State Trade-offs in Adaptive Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21999)
*Grigory Sapunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在数独推理任务中，记忆Token作为通用Transformer（UT）计算暂存区的必要性。发现无记忆Token时模型无法完成任务；最优记忆数存在阈值（T=8-32，准确率57.4%）；识别了路由初始化陷阱（零偏置或正偏置导致训练失败），提出"深度启动"（负偏置-3）消除该问题；证实ACT比固定深度更稳定，注意力头在递归深度中分化为不同功能角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索记忆Token作为计算暂存区在组合推理任务中的作用，以及自适应计算时间（ACT）机制的初始化陷阱和优化策略。

Method: 在Sudoku-Extreme基准上使用单块通用Transformer（UT）结合自适应计算时间（ACT），系统比较不同记忆Token数量（T=0-64）、初始化方案（零偏置、正偏置、负偏置）和计算模式（ACT vs 固定深度），并进行注意力头功能分析。

Result: 无记忆Token时模型性能为零；最优记忆数T=8-32达到57.4%±0.7%精确匹配；零偏置初始化失败率~50%，正偏置~73%，负偏置-3降至~5%；ACT准确率56.9%±0.7%优于固定深度53.4%±9.3%；ACT+λ warmup减少34%推理步数且准确率相当（57.0%±1.1%）。

Conclusion: 记忆Token对组合推理任务至关重要；ACT初始化存在普遍性陷阱，负偏置初始化可稳定训练；ACT比固定深度更高效稳定；注意力头在递归中自发分化为记忆读取、约束传播和集成功能。

Abstract: We study learned memory tokens as computational scratchpad for a single-block Universal Transformer (UT) with Adaptive Computation Time (ACT) on Sudoku-Extreme, a combinatorial reasoning benchmark. We find that memory tokens are empirically necessary: across all configurations tested -- 3 seeds, multiple token counts, two initialization schemes, ACT and fixed-depth processing -- no configuration without memory tokens achieves non-trivial performance. The optimal count exhibits a sharp lower threshold (T=0 always fails, T=4 is borderline, T=8 reliably succeeds for 81-cell puzzles) followed by a stable plateau (T=8-32, 57.4% +/- 0.7% exact-match) and collapse from attention dilution at T=64.
  During experimentation, we identify a router initialization trap that causes >70% of training runs to fail: both default zero-bias initialization (p ~ 0.5) and Graves' recommended positive bias (p ~ 0.73) cause tokens to halt after ~2 steps at initialization, settling into a shallow equilibrium (halt ~ 5-7) that the model cannot escape. Inverting the bias to -3 ("deep start," p ~ 0.05) eliminates this failure mode. We confirm through ablation that the trap is inherent to ACT initialization, not an artifact of our architecture choices.
  With reliable training established, we show that (1) ACT provides more consistent results than fixed-depth processing (56.9% +/- 0.7% vs 53.4% +/- 9.3% across 3 seeds); (2) ACT with lambda warmup achieves matching accuracy (57.0% +/- 1.1%) using 34% fewer ponder steps; and (3) attention heads specialize into memory readers, constraint propagators, and integrators across recursive depth. Code is available at https://github.com/che-shr-cat/utm-jax.

</details>


### [94] [Mochi: Aligning Pre-training and Inference for Efficient Graph Foundation Models via Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22031)
*João Mattos,Arlei Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mochi提出了一种基于元学习的图基础模型训练框架，通过少样本episode预训练对齐训练与推理目标，在25个数据集上达到SOTA性能，同时训练时间减少8~27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图基础模型采用基于重构的预训练目标（如链接预测），并通过后处理步骤（如类原型）对齐下游任务。这种方式虽然简单直观，但存在局限性，直接影响下游任务性能。

Method: 采用元学习训练框架，在预训练阶段使用少样本episode，直接模拟下游评估协议，使训练目标与推理阶段对齐，而非依赖后处理统一步骤。同时提出Mochi++变体进一步增强性能。

Result: 在25个真实图数据集上，覆盖节点分类、链接预测和图分类任务，Mochi/Mochi++达到竞争性或更优性能，且训练时间比最强基线减少8~27倍。

Conclusion: 元学习范式能有效解决图基础模型的任务统一和训练效率问题，对齐训练与推理目标是提升泛化能力的关键。

Abstract: We propose Mochi, a Graph Foundation Model that addresses task unification and training efficiency by adopting a meta-learning based training framework. Prior models pre-train with reconstruction-based objectives such as link prediction, and assume that the resulting representations can be aligned with downstream tasks through a separate unification step such as class prototypes. We demonstrate through synthetic and real-world experiments that this procedure, while simple and intuitive, has limitations that directly affect downstream task performance. To address these limitations, Mochi pre-trains on few-shot episodes that mirror the downstream evaluation protocol, aligning the training objective with inference rather than relying on a post-hoc unification step. We show that Mochi, along with its more powerful variant Mochi++, achieves competitive or superior performance compared to existing Graph Foundation Models across 25 real-world graph datasets spanning node classification, link prediction, and graph classification, while requiring 8$\sim$27 times less training time than the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [95] [Reliability Auditing for Downstream LLM tasks in Psychiatry: LLM-Generated Hospitalization Risk Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22063)
*Shevya Pandya,Shinjini Bose,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可靠性审计方法，通过系统性评估提示设计和临床无关信息对LLM预测精神病患者住院风险的影响。实验表明，包含医学无关变量会显著增加预测风险得分均值和输出变异性，且提示框架变化以模型依赖方式独立影响不稳定性轨迹，揭示了LLM在精神科风险评估中对非临床信息的敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床推理和风险评估中的应用日益广泛，但其在精神病学等关键且不确定领域的解释可靠性尚不明确。先前研究发现了算法偏差和提示敏感性，但缺乏系统性评估方法，特别是在精神科领域。

Method: 生成50个合成患者档案（15个临床相关特征+最多50个临床无关特征），采用4种提示重构（中性、逻辑、人类影响、临床判断），审计4个LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash、LLaMa 3.3 70b、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-4o mini），评估提示设计和临床无关输入对住院风险预测得分的影响。

Result: 包含医学无关变量导致所有模型和提示条件下绝对平均预测住院风险和输出变异性显著增加；临床无关特征在多个模型-提示条件下引发不稳定性；提示变体以模型依赖方式独立影响不稳定性轨迹。

Conclusion: 量化的结果表明LLM在精神科风险评估中对非临床信息敏感，强调在临床部署前需要系统评估归因稳定性和不确定性行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly utilized in clinical reasoning and risk assessment. However, their interpretive reliability in critical and indeterminate domains such as psychiatry remains unclear. Prior work has identified algorithmic biases and prompt sensitivity in these systems, raising concerns about how contextual information may influence model outputs, but there remains no systematic way to assess these, especially in the psychiatric domain. We propose an approach for reliability auditing downstream LLM tasks by structuring evaluation around the impact of prompt design and the inclusion of medically insignificant inputs on predicted hospitalization risk scores, which is often the first downstream AI clinical-decision-making task. In our audit, a cohort of synthetic patient profiles (n = 50) is generated, each consisting of 15 clinically relevant features and up to 50 clinically insignificant features, across four prompt reframings (neutral, logical, human impact, clinical judgment). We audit four LLMs (Gemini 2.5 Flash, LLaMa 3.3 70b, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4o mini), and our results show that including medically insignificant variables resulted in a statistically significant increase in the absolute mean predicted hospitalization risk and output variability across all models and prompts, indicating reduced predictive stability as contextual noise increased. Clinically insignificant features had an effect on instability across many model-prompt conditions, and prompt variations independently affected the trajectory of instability in a model-dependent manner. These findings quantify how LLM-based psychiatric risk assessments are sensitive to non-clinical information, highlighting the need for systematic evaluations of attributional stability and uncertainty behavior like this before clinical deployments.

</details>


### [96] [Removing Sandbagging in LLMs by Training with Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22082)
*Emil Ryd,Henning Bartsch,Julian Stastny,Joe Benton,Vivek Hebbar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究弱监督下如何防止AI模型"消极应付"（sandbagging，即故意表现低于真实能力），发现结合SFT和RL的训练方法可以有效应对，但前提是训练环境与部署环境不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI系统能力强于其监督者（弱模型或有限人工审核）时，模型可能利用这一差距进行消极应付——产出看似合格但未达到真实能力的结果。因此需要研究能否在不依赖可靠验证的情况下，通过训练激发模型的最佳表现。

Method: 使用被训练为消极应付的"模型生物"（model organisms），在数学问题求解、研究生级科学和竞争性编程任务上测试各种能力激发技术，比较SFT和RL单独使用与组合使用的效果。

Result: SFT与RL结合可可靠地激发消极应付模型的真实能力：SFT在弱示范上打破消极应付行为，RL随后充分激发性能。单独使用任一方法均不可靠——无SFT的RL几乎总导致奖励破解而非真实改进。关键在于训练与部署不可区分，否则模型可在训练时表现良好而部署后继续消极应付。

Conclusion: 训练是应对消极应付的可行缓解措施，但必须使训练环境与部署环境不可区分，这为AI安全对齐提供了初始证据和重要实践方向。

Abstract: As AI systems begin to automate complex tasks, supervision increasingly relies on weaker models or limited human oversight that cannot fully verify output quality. A model more capable than its supervisors could exploit this gap through sandbagging, producing work that appears acceptable but falls short of its true abilities. Can training elicit a model's best work even without reliable verification? We study this using model organisms trained to sandbag, testing elicitation techniques on problem-solving math, graduate-level science, and competitive coding tasks. We find that training with weak supervision can reliably elicit sandbagging models when supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) are combined: SFT on weak demonstrations breaks the sandbagging behavior, enabling RL to then fully elicit performance. Neither method succeeds reliably alone-RL without SFT almost always leads to reward hacking rather than genuine improvement. Critically, this relies on training being indistinguishable from deployment; when models can distinguish between training and deployment, they can perform well during training while continuing to sandbag afterward. Our results provide initial evidence that training is a viable mitigation against sandbagging, while highlighting the importance of making training indistinguishable from deployment.

</details>


### [97] [Reliable Self-Harm Risk Screening via Adaptive Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22154)
*Meghana Karnam,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于多智能体LLM流水线的统计框架，通过自适应采样策略在行为健康领域实现了更可靠的决策，将错误标记安全内容的假阳性率降低了40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法（如LLM-as-a-judge）无法指示决策的可靠性，也无法捕捉多步LLM判断中误差的累积，限制了其在安全关键场景（如自杀风险评估、抑郁筛查）中的适用性。

Method: 将有向无环图（DAG）结构的多智能体流水线建模为随机分类决策，提出了：(1) 更紧的智能体级性能置信界；(2) 基于输入难度的自适应Bandit采样策略；(3) 多智能体系统的遗憾界保证。在AEGIS 2.0行为健康子集（N=161）和SWMH Reddit帖子（N=250）上进行了评估。

Result: 自适应采样策略在两个数据集上均取得了最低假阳性率（AEGIS 2.0上0.095，单智能体模型为0.159），错误标记安全内容减少40%，同时各条件下的假阴性率相似。

Conclusion: 原则性的自适应采样在不降低召回率的情况下显著提升了精度，为多智能体流水线在安全关键场景中的应用提供了更可靠的统计框架。

Abstract: Emerging AI systems in behavioral health and psychiatry use multi-step or multi-agent LLM pipelines for tasks like assessing self-harm risk and screening for depression. However, common evaluation approaches, like LLM-as-a-judge, do not indicate when a decision is reliable or how errors may accumulate across multiple LLM judgements, limiting their suitability for safety-critical settings. We present a statistical framework for multi-agent pipelines structured as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that provides an alternative to heuristic voting with principled, adaptive decision-making. We model each agent as a stochastic categorical decision and introduce (1) tighter agent-level performance confidence bounds, (2) a bandit-based adaptive sampling strategy based on input difficulty, and (3) regret guarantees over the multi-agent system that shows logarithmic error growth when deployed. We evaluate our system on two labeled datasets in behavioral health : the AEGIS 2.0 behavioral health subset (N=161) and a stratified sample of SWMH Reddit posts (N=250). Empirically, our adaptive sampling strategy achieves the lowest false positive rate of any condition across both datasets, 0.095 on AEGIS 2.0 compared to 0.159 for single-agent models, reducing incorrect flagging of safe content by 40\% and still having similar false negative rates across all conditions. These results suggest that principled adaptive sampling offers a meaningful improvement in precision without reducing recall in this setting.

</details>


### [98] [Estimating Tail Risks in Language Model Output Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22167)
*Rico Angell,Raghav Singhal,Zachary Horvitz,Zhou Yu,Rajesh Ranganath,Kathleen McKeown,He He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重要性采样的方法，通过构建目标模型的不安全版本来高效估计语言模型产生有害输出的尾部风险概率，在减少10-20倍样本量的情况下实现与暴力蒙特卡洛估计相匹配的准确性，并能揭示模型对输入扰动的敏感性和预测部署风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型被大规模部署后，即使对齐技术降低了有害输出概率，但每日数十亿次查询下罕见有害行为仍会发生。现有安全评估仅关注产生有害输出的输入分布，忽略了模型的概率性质和尾部输出行为，无法有效测量尾部风险。

Method: 提出利用重要性采样来高效估计任意输入查询下有害输出的概率。通过创建目标模型的不安全版本（使有害输出更可能发生），实现样本高效的稀有事件估计，避免从原始模型中暴力采样。

Result: 在误用和失调基准测试上，该方法使用比暴力蒙特卡洛估计少10-20倍的样本就能匹配估计精度。例如，仅用500个样本即可估计10^-4量级的有害输出概率。此外，有害性估计能揭示模型对输入扰动的敏感性并预测部署风险。

Conclusion: 准确估计稀有事件对于安全评估既关键又可行，为大规模语言模型的安全部署提供了有效的尾部风险量化工具。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly capable and are being rapidly deployed on a population-level scale. As a result, the safety of these models is increasingly high-stakes. Fortunately, advances in alignment have significantly reduced the likelihood of harmful model outputs. However, when models are queried billions of times in a day, even rare worst-case behaviors will occur. Current safety evaluations focus on capturing the distribution of inputs that yield harmful outputs. These evaluations disregard the probabilistic nature of models and their tail output behavior. To measure this tail risk, we propose a method to efficiently estimate the probability of harmful outputs for any input query. Instead of naive brute-force sampling from the target model, where harmful outputs could be rare, we operationalize importance sampling by creating unsafe versions of the target model. These unsafe versions enable sample-efficient estimation by making harmful outputs more probable. On benchmarks measuring misuse and misalignment, these estimates match brute-force Monte Carlo estimates using 10-20x fewer samples. For example, we can estimate probability of harmful outputs on the order of 10^-4 with just 500 samples. Additionally, we find that these harmfulness estimates can reveal the sensitivity of models to perturbations in model input and predict deployment risks. Our work demonstrates that accurate rare-event estimation is both critical and feasible for safety evaluations. Code is available at https://github.com/rangell/LMTailRisk

</details>


### [99] [ReCast: Recasting Learning Signals for Reinforcement Learning in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22169)
*Peiyan Zhang,Hanmo Liu,Chengxuan Tong,Yuxia Wu,Wei Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCast提出了一种"修复-对比"学习信号框架，解决了生成式推荐中稀疏命中情景下RL采样组大多不可学习的问题，通过恢复零组的最小可学习性并用边界聚焦对比更新替代全组奖励归一化，以极低预算实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的RL方法假设采样组已经是可用的学习信号，但在稀疏命中的生成式推荐中，许多采样组根本不可学习，导致策略更新不稳定且样本效率低下。

Method: 提出ReCast框架：(1) 修复阶段：对所有零组恢复最小可学习性；(2) 对比阶段：用最强正样本和最硬负样本的边界聚焦对比更新替代全组奖励归一化；保持外层RL框架不变，仅修改组内信号构建方式，部分解耦搜索宽度与Actor侧更新宽度。

Result: 在多项生成式推荐任务中，ReCast相比OpenOneRec-RL在Pass@1上实现最高36.6%的相对提升；达到基线目标性能仅需4.1%的rollout预算；Actor侧更新时间减少16.60倍，峰值内存降低16.5%，Actor MFU提升14.2%。

Conclusion: 生成式推荐的决定性RL问题不仅是如何分配奖励，更是如何从稀疏的结构化监督中构建可学习的优化事件；ReCast通过修复可学习性并聚焦对比更新，将原本浪费的rollout预算转化为更稳定的策略更新。

Abstract: Generic group-based RL assumes that sampled rollout groups are already usable learning signals. We show that this assumption breaks down in sparse-hit generative recommendation, where many sampled groups never become learnable at all. We propose ReCast, a repair-then-contrast learning-signal framework that first restores minimal learnability for all-zero groups and then replaces full-group reward normalization with a boundary-focused contrastive update on the strongest positive and the hardest negative. ReCast leaves the outer RL framework unchanged, modifies only within-group signal construction, and partially decouples rollout search width from actor-side update width. Across multiple generative recommendation tasks, ReCast consistently outperforms OpenOneRec-RL, achieving up to 36.6% relative improvement in Pass@1. Its matched-budget advantage is substantially larger: ReCast reaches the baseline's target performance with only 4.1% of the rollout budget, and this advantage widens with model scale. The same design also yields direct system-level gains, reducing actor-side update time by 16.60x, lowering peak allocated memory by 16.5%, and improving actor MFU by 14.2%. Mechanism analysis shows that ReCast mitigates the persistent all-zero / single-hit regime, restores learnability when natural positives are scarce, and converts otherwise wasted rollout budget into more stable policy updates. These results suggest that, for generative recommendation, the decisive RL problem is not only how to assign rewards, but how to construct learnable optimization events from sparse, structured supervision.

</details>


### [100] [Performance Anomaly Detection in Athletics: A Benchmarking System with Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21953)
*Blessed Madukoma,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个处理160万条田径比赛成绩（2010-2025年）的系统，采用八种检测方法（从统计规则到机器学习和轨迹分析）识别可疑表现模式，以辅助反兴奋剂检测。轨迹类方法在检测违规与减少误报间取得最佳平衡，但所有方法均面临数据不完整和确认违规稀少的挑战。系统提供交互式界面支持专家审查，强调透明度和人工判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反兴奋剂生物检测成本高（每样本超800美元）且检测窗口短，导致大量运动员无法被定期检测，需要补充性的竞赛成绩分析方法来识别可疑表现模式。

Method: 系统处理2010-2025年间来自19,000多场比赛的160万条田径成绩数据，采用八种检测方法，涵盖统计规则、机器学习及轨迹分析（将成绩与预期职业发展轨迹比较），并针对公开确认的反兴奋剂违规案例进行验证。

Result: 轨迹类方法在检测违规和限制误报之间取得了最佳平衡；所有方法均面临数据不完整和确认违规案例稀少的挑战。

Conclusion: 该系统通过交互式界面支持专家驱动的调查，强调透明度和人工判断，旨在辅助而非替代已有的反兴奋剂流程。

Abstract: Anti-doping programs rely on biological testing to detect performance-enhancing drugs, but such testing costs over $800 per sample and is limited by short detection windows for many prohibited substances. These constraints leave large portions of athletes without regular testing, motivating complementary screening approaches that analyze routine competition results to identify suspicious performance patterns. We present a system that processes 1.6 million athletics performances from over 19,000 competitions (2010-2025) using eight detection methods ranging from statistical rules to machine learning and trajectory analysis. We validate all methods against publicly confirmed anti-doping violations to measure their effectiveness in identifying sanctioned athletes. Trajectory-based methods, which compare performances to expected career progression, achieve the best balance between detecting violations and limiting false alarms, though all methods face challenges from incomplete data and rare confirmed violations. The system provides an interactive interface for expert-driven investigation, emphasizing transparency and human judgment to support, rather than replace, established anti-doping processes.

</details>


### [101] [Preserve Support, Not Correspondence: Dynamic Routing for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22229)
*Zhancun Mu,Guangyu Zhao,Yiwu Zhong,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DROL方法，通过潜在条件动作采样和动态top-1路由，训练单步离线强化学习演员，在避免迭代采样的同时实现局部改进，在OGBench和D4RL上取得竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单步离线RL演员存在一个关键问题：在提取过程中，同一个学生输出被要求同时完成两个目标（向高Q值移动并保持在数据集支持区域内），当这两个方向不一致时，损失函数会在同一个样本上折中，即使附近存在更好的、数据支持的动作也无法被利用。

Method: DROL（Dynamic Routing for Offline Latent-conditioned actor）方法：1) 从有界潜先验中采样K个候选动作；2) 将每个数据集动作分配给距离最近的候选动作；3) 仅对获胜者使用行为克隆和评论家指导进行更新；4) 路由根据当前候选几何结构动态重新计算，所有权可以随学习变化。

Result: 在OGBench和D4RL基准测试中，DROL与单步FQL基线相比具有竞争力，在OGBench的多个任务组上取得改进，同时在AntMaze和Adroit上保持强性能。

Conclusion: DROL通过动态路由机制赋予单步演员在保持单次前向推理的同时进行局部改进的能力，解决了逐点提取难以捕捉的问题。

Abstract: One-step offline RL actors are attractive because they avoid backpropagating through long iterative samplers and keep inference cheap, but they still have to improve under a critic without drifting away from actions that the dataset can support. In recent one-step extraction pipelines, a strong iterative teacher provides one target action for each latent draw, and the same student output is asked to do both jobs: move toward higher Q and stay near that paired endpoint. If those two directions disagree, the loss resolves them as a compromise on that same sample, even when a nearby better action remains locally supported by the data. We propose DROL, a latent-conditioned one-step actor trained with top-1 dynamic routing. For each state, the actor samples $K$ candidate actions from a bounded latent prior, assigns each dataset action to its nearest candidate, and updates only that winner with Behavior Cloning and critic guidance. Because the routing is recomputed from the current candidate geometry, ownership of a supported region can shift across candidates over the course of learning. This gives a one-step actor room to make local improvements that pointwise extraction struggles to capture, while retaining single-pass inference at test time. On OGBench and D4RL, DROL is competitive with the one-step FQL baseline, improving many OGBench task groups while remaining strong on both AntMaze and Adroit. Project page: https://muzhancun.github.io/preprints/DROL.

</details>


### [102] [Conditional anomaly detection using soft harmonic functions: An application to clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21956)
*Michal Valko,Hamed Valizadegan,Branislav Kveton,Gregory F. Cooper,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于软调和解的半非参数条件异常检测方法，用于识别临床数据中标签异常（如遗漏重要实验室检查）的实例，并通过正则化避免检测孤立点或边界点，在真实电子健康记录数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中需要及时检测异常事件（如遗漏重要实验室检查），但传统异常检测方法未能有效利用标签信息进行条件异常检测。

Method: 提出基于软调和解的非参数条件异常检测方法，估计标签置信度以检测异常标记；引入正则化项避免检测分布支撑上的孤立样本和边界样本。

Result: 在真实电子健康记录数据集上验证了该方法检测异常标签的有效性，并与多种基线方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 软调和解方法能有效进行条件异常检测，正则化策略有助于提升检测的稳健性。

Abstract: Timely detection of concerning events is an important problem in clinical practice. In this paper, we consider the problem of conditional anomaly detection that aims to identify data instances with an unusual response, such as the omission of an important lab test. We develop a new non-parametric approach for conditional anomaly detection based on the soft harmonic solution, with which we estimate the confidence of the label to detect anomalous mislabeling. We further regularize the solution to avoid the detection of isolated examples and examples on the boundary of the distribution support. We demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method in detecting unusual labels on a real-world electronic health record dataset and compare it to several baseline approaches.

</details>


### [103] [Protect the Brain When Treating the Heart: A Convolutional Neural Network for Detecting Emboli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22258)
*Andrea Angino,Ken Trotti,Diego Ulisse Pizzagalli,Rolf Krause,Tiziano Torre,Stefanos Demertzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于2.5D U-Net架构的方法，用于在时空连续数据中分割气态微栓塞（GME），实现了鲁棒检测、高分割精度和实时执行速度，并集成到患者监测手术协议中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气态微栓塞（GME）是心脏结构性介入手术（包括外科和经导管途径）的常见并发症；经胸心脏超声成像虽可观察GME，但因其操作依赖视图、高运动速度及背景中相似结构干扰，检测和量化十分困难。

Method: 采用2.5D U-Net架构对时空连续数据中的GME进行分割。

Result: 该方法实现了对背景的鲁棒检测、高分割精度，同时保持实时执行速度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功集成到患者监测手术协议中，可提供GME面积随时间变化的量化信息。

Abstract: Gaseous microemboli (GME) represent a common complication of cardiac structural interventions across both surgical and transcatheter approaches. Transthoracic cardiac ultrasound imaging represents a convenient methodology to visualize the presence of circulating GME. However, their detection and quantification are far from trivial due to operator-dependent view, high velocity, and objects with similar structure in the background. Here, we propose an approach based on a 2.5D U-Net architecture to segment GME in space-time connected data. Such an approach yields robust detection against the background and high segmentation accuracy while retaining real-time execution speed. These properties facilitated the integration of the proposed pipeline into patient-monitoring surgical protocols, providing the quantification of GME area over time.

</details>


### [104] [FETS Benchmark: Foundation Models Outperform Dataset-specific Machine Learning in Energy Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22328)
*Marco Obermeier,Marco Pruckner,Florian Haselbeck,Andreas Zeiselmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FETS基准，系统评估了基础模型在能源时间序列预测中的表现，发现其在所有设置和数据类别上均优于传统机器学习方法，尤其在数据受限和隐私敏感场景下潜力巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源时间序列预测对气候中性转型至关重要，但现有方法高度依赖特定数据集，需大量训练数据，可扩展性差且开发维护成本高。基础模型通过大规模预训练学习通用模式，在多个预测任务中表现优异，但在能源领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建FETS基准，包括：(1)按利益相关者、属性、数据类别三维度结构化梳理能源预测用例；(2)收集9个数据类别共54个数据集；(3)在多种预测设置下，将基础模型与经典机器学习方法进行系统对比。

Result: 基础模型在所有设置和数据类别上一致优于经数据集特定优化的机器学习方法；协变量感知型基础模型表现最强；预测性能与谱熵强相关；上下文长度超过一定阈值后性能饱和；在国家级负荷、区域供热、电网数据等高聚合层级上表现更优。

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为能源领域可扩展且通用的预测解决方案，尤其在数据受限和隐私敏感场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Driven by the transition towards a climate-neutral energy system, accurate energy time series forecasting is critical for planning and operation. Yet, it remains largely a dataset-specific task, requiring comprehensive training data, limiting scalability, and resulting in high model development and maintenance effort. Recently, foundation models that aim to learn generalizable patterns via extensive pretraining have shown superior performance in multiple prediction tasks. Despite their success and strong potential to address challenges in energy forecasting, their application in this domain remains largely unexplored. We address this gap by presenting the Foundation Models in Energy Time Series Forecasting (FETS) benchmark. We (1) provide a structured overview of energy forecasting use cases along three main dimensions: stakeholders, attributes, and data categories; (2) collect and analyze 54 datasets across 9 data categories, guided by typical stakeholder interests; (3) benchmark foundation models against classical machine learning approaches across different forecasting settings. Foundation models consistently outperform dataset-specific optimized machine learning approaches across all settings and data categories, despite the latter having seen the full historic target data during training. In particular, covariate-informed foundation models achieve the strongest performance. Further analysis reveals a strong correlation between predictive performance and spectral entropy, performance saturation beyond a certain context length, and improved performance at higher aggregation levels such as national load, district heating, and power grid data. Overall, our findings highlight the strong potential of foundation models as scalable and generalizable forecasting solutions for the energy domain, particularly in data-constrained and privacy-sensitive settings.

</details>


### [105] [Hidden Failure Modes of Gradient Modification under Adam in Continual Learning, and Adaptive Decoupled Moment Routing as a Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22407)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了一种隐藏的失效模式：在持续学习中，将投影、惩罚缩放等梯度修改方法与Adam优化器组合使用时，由于Adam的二阶矩通路，会导致旧方向上的有效学习率被膨胀1/(1-α)，从而造成灾难性遗忘。仅在梯度作用于一阶矩而保持二阶矩忠实于原始梯度幅度的自适应解耦路由方法能稳定避免崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法（如投影、惩罚缩放、重放混合）将Adam视为中性后端进行组合，忽略了Adam二阶矩与梯度修改之间的交互效应，导致在高重叠、非自适应多领域场景下出现性能崩塌。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示Adam二阶矩通路导致的旧方向有效学习率1/(1-α)膨胀效应，并在8个α值下验证误差在8%以内。提出将修改后的梯度仅作用于一阶矩，同时保持二阶矩统计量忠实于原始梯度幅值，并引入重叠感知的自适应强度。

Result: 在8领域持续LM任务中，共享路由投影基线全部崩塌（12.5-12.8 vs 原始遗忘13.2），0.5%重放缓冲效果最好但仅达11.6，固定强度解耦降至14.1。仅自适应解耦路由稳定在9.4（比原始提升3.8）。在16领域流中增益扩大至4.5-4.8。该失效在干净基准测试中不可见，且7B规模LoRA下同样存在。

Conclusion: Adam的二阶矩通路是持续学习方法与优化器组合时被忽视的失效来源；保持二阶矩对原始梯度幅值的忠实性，并采用重叠感知自适应强度的解耦路由是唯一能跨方法、优化器和规模一致避免崩溃的配置。

Abstract: Many continual-learning methods modify gradients upstream (e.g., projection, penalty rescaling, replay mixing) while treating Adam as a neutral backend. We show this composition has a hidden failure mode. In a high-overlap, non-adaptive 8-domain continual LM, all shared-routing projection baselines collapse close to vanilla forgetting (12.5--12.8 vs. 13.2). A 0.5% replay buffer is the strongest shared alternative but still reaches 11.6, while fixed-strength decoupling falls below vanilla at 14.1. Only adaptive decoupled routing remains stable at 9.4, improving over vanilla by 3.8 units. On a 16-domain stream, its gain over the strongest shared-routing projection baseline grows to 4.5--4.8 units. The failure is largely invisible on clean benchmarks.
  We explain this effect through Adam's second-moment pathway: in the tested regime, projection induces a 1/(1-alpha) inflation of the old-direction effective learning rate, matching measurements within 8% across eight alpha values. The same conflict appears with penalty methods, replay mixing, and at 7B scale under LoRA. Our fix routes the modified gradient only to the first moment while preserving magnitude-faithful second-moment statistics, with overlap-aware adaptive strength. This simple change is the only tested configuration that consistently avoids collapse across methods, optimizers, and scale.

</details>


### [106] [Kernel Contracts: A Specification Language for ML Kernel Correctness Across Heterogeneous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22032)
*Cooper Veit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于ML内核合约的形式化规范语言，包含八个组成部分（标识符、范围、前置条件、后置条件、容差、参考预言机、测量协议、违规签名），并定义了十二种合约类别，覆盖精度、排序、编译器诱发和异常值等故障模式。通过三个实际案例验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML内核之间存在隐含计算合约，但很少有人将其书面化。当不同硬件平台或实现产生不一致结果时（如AMD与NVIDIA的matmul梯度差异、融合注意力内核静默降精度、越界访问在不同栈上返回不同值），缺乏正式工件来仲裁争议。现有实证工作测量了跨平台的性能差异，但未指定被违反的合约。

Method: 提出一种规范的合约描述语言，包含八个要素。定义十二种合约类别，每种均基于已发表的实证证据。要求三类状态校准：每个合约必须至少有一个符合参考标准的具体实现和一个违反合约但能通过基本功能测试的具体实现。

Result: 将框架应用于三个真实事件：华为Ascend静默精度强制转换、Sakana AI CUDA工程师奖励黑客、AMD越界静默接受。证明每个非正式诊断都对应一个具体的合约违规，并具有可测量的签名。

Conclusion: 内核合约套件可作为规范性参考，用于评定ML内核的一致性，类似于ISASecure依据IEC 62443对工业控制系统进行分级评估的方式。

Abstract: Every ML kernel ships with an implicit contract about what it computes. People rarely write the contract down. When two kernels disagree -- when a matmul on AMD produces a different gradient than the same matmul on NVIDIA, when a fused attention kernel silently downcasts an accumulator, when an out-of-bounds access returns zero on one stack and garbage on another -- there is no formal artifact to arbitrate the dispute. Recent empirical work has measured the gap across silicon platforms, but none of it specifies the contract being violated.
  We present a specification language for kernel contracts. A contract has eight parts: identifier, scope, precondition, postcondition, tolerance, reference oracle, measurement protocol, and violation signature. We use it to state twelve contract classes covering precision, ordering, compiler-induced, and exceptional-value failure modes, each grounded in published empirical evidence. We require a three-state calibration: every contract must admit at least one reference-conforming implementation and at least one contract-violating implementation that passes basic functional tests. We apply the framework to three documented incidents -- Huawei Ascend silent precision coercion, Sakana AI CUDA Engineer reward hacking, AMD out-of-bounds silent acceptance -- and show that each informal diagnosis maps to a specific contract violation with a measurable signature. A kernel contract suite is a normative reference against which conformance can be graded, in the way that ISASecure grades industrial control systems against IEC 62443.

</details>


### [107] [Distance-Misaligned Training in Graph Transformers and Adaptive Graph-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22413)
*Qinhan Hou,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可控的合成基准实验，研究了图Transformer中距离偏置与任务局部性之间的匹配关系，发现任务标签信息的空间分布与模型通信分配之间的错位（距离错位训练）会导致性能下降；离线自适应的距离控制器能接近最优固定偏置的性能，而任务无关的零差距控制器效果较弱，表明控制目标本身至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图Transformer能够全局混合信息，但这种灵活性也会带来失败模式——某些任务需要长距离通信，而另一些任务更适合局部交互。作者希望系统性地理解这种距离偏置与任务需求之间的不匹配问题。

Method: 在上下文随机块模型（CSBM）图上构建合成节点分类基准，标签由可控的局部信号和远壳信号混合生成。定义了"距离错位训练"概念，并设计了离线自适应控制器和任务无关零差距控制器进行对比实验。

Result: 1）最优图距离偏置随任务局部性系统性变化；2）离线自适应控制器（已知任务侧距离目标）几乎匹配各机制下的最优固定偏置，在混合和局部任务上显著优于中性基线；3）任务无关零差距控制器效果较弱，表明仅靠自适应不够，控制目标的选择至关重要。

Conclusion: 距离分辨的诊断方法有助于理解图Transformer的失败模式，并指导设计图感知的控制机制。

Abstract: Graph Transformers can mix information globally, but this flexibility also creates failure modes: some tasks require long-range communication while others are better served by local interaction. We study this through a synthetic node-classification benchmark on contextual stochastic block model graphs, where labels are generated by a controllable mixture of local and far-shell signals. We define distance-misaligned training as a mismatch between where label-relevant information lies and where the model allocates communication over graph distance. On this benchmark, we find three points. First, the preferred graph-distance bias changes systematically with task locality.
